Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on systems structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 236. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 319 with relations in degree 5.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_236(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 236. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^319(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 217.2 (Structural Decomposition)
